country_event = {

	desc = cosmo_consortduo_events.4001.d

			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1
			}
		}

			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_2
			}

	}
	picture = {

			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_3
			}			
		has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_2
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1_eventPicture
	}

			}

		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_5
			}		
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_2_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {	
		has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_5
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_6
			}		
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1_eventPicture
	}
	
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_consortduo_events.4001.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
		has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_a			
		 }	
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1		
       }	
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_2
						NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_3
			}			
		}

       }
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_4
						NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_5
			}			
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_5
       }
		set_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_a
		
			cosmo_consort_artist_effect = yes												

		remove_country_modifier = having_an_affair
		
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_ruler_flag = ruler_having_affair				

			random_list = {
			8 = { #bastard girl
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_symptoms_tooltip
			}
			8 = { #bastard boy
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_symptoms_tooltip
			}
			4 = { #std
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_symptoms_tooltip
			}			
			80 = { #no std
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_symptoms_tooltip 

			}			
			}	   
		}		
		}		
		
		option = {
		name = cosmo_consortduo_events.4001.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_a			
		}

			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1

			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_3

			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_4
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_4
       }
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1	
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_2		
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_3	
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_4
			has_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_5			
		}
		clr_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_1
		clr_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_2
		clr_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_3
		clr_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_4
		clr_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_5		
       }	   
		clr_country_flag = DUO_CONSORT_EUROPE_15A_EAST_SLAVIC_ARTIST_a
			cosmo_consort_artist_effect = yes
